Inconsistent agent config/environment is a problem: 1. Work in declarative, ephemeral sandboxes (e.g. Docker containers). 2. Put everything possible (skills/plugins/configs/markdown) in the repo itself. 3. Use a system that enables running agents with team-shared configs and shared agent memory.

## **70. Post by `@mackuba.eu` on 2026-09-04**

<div class="bsky-card" markdown="1">

<div class="bsky-header" markdown="1">

<img src="https://cdn.bsky.app/img/avatar/plain/did:plc:oio4hkxaop4ao4wz2pp3f4cr/bafkreidcvmjwmk24rfnmdwgcnwx7i746revfkvvmtknemrr3uc4pjqmbgu" class="bsky-avatar">

<div class="bsky-author-meta" markdown="1">
**Kuba Suder 🇵🇱🇺🇦** [@mackuba.eu](https://bsky.app/profile/did:plc:oio4hkxaop4ao4wz2pp3f4cr){:target="_blank"}
<br><small>2026-09-04</small>
</div>
</div>

<div class="bsky-content" markdown="1">

This is one of my favorite API names in Ruby ❤️ (•̀ᢍ•́)



<div class="bsky-image-grid bsky-grid-1" markdown="1">



![chomp(line_sep = $/) → new_string Returns a new string copied from self, with trailing characters possibly removed:  When line_sep is &#34;\n&#34;, removes the last one or two characters if they are &#34;\r&#34;, &#34;\n&#34;, or &#34;\r\n&#34; (but not &#34;\n\r&#34;):  $/                    # =&gt; &#34;\n&#34; &#34;abc\r&#34;.chomp         # =&gt; &#34;abc&#34; &#34;abc\n&#34;.chomp         # =&gt; &#34;abc&#34; &#34;abc\r\n&#34;.chomp       # =&gt; &#34;abc&#34; &#34;abc\n\r&#34;.chomp       # =&gt; &#34;abc\n&#34; &#34;тест\r\n&#34;.chomp      # =&gt; &#34;тест&#34; &#34;こんにちは\r\n&#34;.chomp  # =&gt; &#34;こんにちは&#34; When line_sep is &#39;&#39; (an empty string), removes multiple trailing occurrences of &#34;\n&#34; or &#34;\r\n&#34; (but not &#34;\r&#34; or &#34;\n\r&#34;):  &#34;abc\n\n\n&#34;.chomp(&#39;&#39;)           # =&gt; &#34;abc&#34; &#34;abc\r\n\r\n\r\n&#34;.chomp(&#39;&#39;)     # =&gt; &#34;abc&#34; &#34;abc\n\n\r\n\r\n\n\n&#34;.chomp(&#39;&#39;) # =&gt; &#34;abc&#34; &#34;abc\n\r\n\r\n\r&#34;.chomp(&#39;&#39;)     # =&gt; &#34;abc\n\r\n\r\n\r&#34; &#34;abc\r\r\r&#34;.chomp(&#39;&#39;)           # =&gt; &#34;abc\r\r\r&#34; When line_sep is neither &#34;\n&#34; nor &#39;&#39;, removes a single trailing line separator if there is one:  &#39;abcd&#39;.chomp(&#39;d&#39;)  # =&gt; &#34;abc&#34; &#39;abcdd&#39;.chomp(&#39;d&#39;) # =&gt; &#34;abcd&#34;](https://cdn.bsky.app/img/feed_fullsize/plain/did:plc:oio4hkxaop4ao4wz2pp3f4cr/bafkreiamvolermna42msukvepavjyn5haiej35lity5meifvsh6fuof44y){ .bsky-img }


</div>





</div>

<div class="bsky-content" markdown="1">
